# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-26T13:13:01+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

As demand for European launch services continues to grow, expanding launch infrastructure is becoming increasingly important. Through their collaboration, SUAS Aerospace and OHB&#8217;s dedicated launch infrastructure subsidiary, TESC (The European Spaceport Company), aim to support the development of additional launch opportunities in Europe and strengthen the continent&#8217;s space transportation ecosystem.   https://www.ohb.de/en/news/ohb-and-suas-aerospace-sign-agreement-to-advance-development-of-european-launch-infrastructure-in-ireland
- undated — Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission
  Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission - ohb.de Skip to content Cleaning up space: OHB awarded EUR 102 million contract by ESA for Europe’s first active space debris removal mission Bremen, November 17, 2026 Low Earth Orbit (LEO) is becoming increasingly congested as the amount of space debris continues to grow. Thousands of defunct satellites and millions of debris fragments orbit Earth, increasing the risk of collisions. With the ClearSpace-1 mission (CS-1), Europe aims to demonstrate for the first time how a satellite can be captured and safely removed from orbit in a controlled manner. For this pioneering mission, the European Space Agency (ESA) has selected OHB System AG as prime contractor. The contract, valued at more than €102 million, was signed today at Space Tech Expo Bremen. &#8220;We are delighted

- undated — Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data This study is a decade-scale demonstration of the value of high-frequency imagery in monitoring and protecting dynamic shorelines.
  Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data HOME Publications Monitoring the Coasts With Near-Daily Satellite Data Austin Stone November 23, 2026 • 3 min read PlanetScope image of the Everglades coastline in Florida, captured May 15, 2025. © 2026, Planet Labs PBC. All Rights reserved. Publications Publications Satellite imagery is a critical resource in monitoring the health of coastal environments that are increasingly vulnerable to the impacts of climate and land use change. However, these ecosystems are so dynamic that many short-lived events can be missed between image captures by satellites that provide resolutions coarser than 10 meters with multiday revisit times, such as Landsat and Sentinel-2. While high-resolution aerial flights or drone surveys offer sub-meter-scale detail, they are costly, localized, and impossible to deploy systematically across entire national coas

Back ‘Trust Me, It’s Gonna Take Off’: Inside Electron’s First 100 Launches Share this Rocket Lab team members at Launch Complex 1 in Māhia, New Zealand, celebrate Electron’s 100th launch. By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days. Even by today's fast-moving aerospace standards, few small launch vehicles have reached even double-digi

  Hera paving the way for a new chapter in planetary defence | Thales Alenia Space Skip to main content Next Thales Alenia Space ... What would happen if an asteroid the size of the Great Pyramid of Giza was on a collision course with Earth? Its impact could destroy entire cities, or generate a devastating tsunami if it struck the ocean. Would humanity be prepared to respond to such a threat? That is precisely what the European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to help determine. On September 26 th 2022, NASA’s Double Asteroid Redirection Test (DART) mission carried out humanity’s first-ever asteroid deflection experiment by deliberately crashing its 580 kg spacecraft into Dimorphos, a moonlet measuring 151 meters in diameter, at a speed of 6.1 km/s.   La Nación / Itaipú busca recuperar millonarios depósitos atrapados en Ueno Bank El director paraguayo de Itaipú, Hugo Zárate , confirmó este jueves que unos USD 200 millones de fondos de la binacional que fueron depositados en Ueno Bank están atrapados. El alto funcionario detalló que, por una parte, se tienen depósitos de Itaipú afectados en cuentas en dólares y guaraníes, que rondan los USD 183 millones, y también algunos depósitos del Parque Tecnológico Itaipú (PTI) que daban cobertura a los proyectos gestionados por esa fundación. Zárate explicó que Ueno Bank fue incluido en la lista de proveedores de servicios financieros de la entidad a partir de una oferta realizada por el propio banco, que fue evaluada por la Dirección Financiera y sometida a un análisis. Para ese análisis se debe ejecutar una instrucción de procedimiento que requiere dar por cumplidos ciertos requisitos fundamen

  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/

  https://payloadspace.com/blacksky-acquires-data-science-firm-epochgeo/

  FCC Approves Elon Musk&#8217;s One Million Orbital Data Centers &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com FCC Chairman Brendan Carr announced today that the FCC has approved Elon Musk&#8217;s request to launch one million satellites to low Earth orbit to serve as data centers in space. Called Starmind, they are in addition to the 42,000 Starlink broadband internet satellites SpaceX plans to deploy plus the military version, Starshield. The vast number of satellites is raising concerns about collisions, atmospheric effects of reentry, and the impact on optical and radio astronomy. SpaceX revealed plans to build one million Orbital Data Centers in January, but it&#8217;s not the only company with sights set on taking advantage of the continuous sunlight available in space to power ODCs.   First Pakistani Astronaut Enroute to Tiangong &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com China launched a new crew to the Tiangong space station this evening EST, Shenzhou-24. Docking is expected about 3.5 hours after liftoff. [ Update : docking was on November 25, 11:56 am Beijing Time (0356 UTC; November 24, 10:56 pm EST).] Launch of Shenzhou-24 was at 7:18 pm Eastern Standard Time, or 8:18 am November 25 local time at China&#8217;s Jiuquan Satellite Launch Center in Inner Mongolia. Credit: Landing Zones Canada. Landing Zones Canada (LZC) and Japan&#8217;s Meisei Electric announced they are flying a joint stratospheric balloon system out of Whitehorse, Yukon, this week. It is the first flight campaign under a partnership the two companies signed in October. The campaign, called HoverEX-1, runs from Nov. 23 to 27 at the Arctic Training Centre, a private site in Whitehorse for testing drones and commercial and defence technology. According to the companies&#8217; announcement , they signed the International Project Consortium Agreement behind it at the Embassy of Canada to Japan in Tokyo on Oct. 1.   USask gets $2.6 million to build Osiris-3 for CSA ozone CubeSat Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content An illustration of the Canadian Space Agency's SCISAT satellite over northern Canada. Launched in 2003, SCISAT is still measuring ozone. Credit: Canadian Space Agency (SCISAT), Shutterstock (Earth). Composite: SpaceQ The Canadian Space Agency (CSA) said on X on Tuesday that it has awarded a $2.6-million contract with the University of Saskatchewan (USask) to develop Osiris-3, a successor to the Canadian OSIRIS instrument that measured ozone from Sweden’s Odin satellite from 2001 until earlier this year. Osiris-3 will measure the spectrum of sunlight scattered by the atmosphere, looking at it edge-on along Earth’s horizon to work out how ozone, nitrogen dioxide and aerosols, the fine particles suspended in the air, vary with altitude. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

- Wed, 25 Nov 2026 12:02:51 +0100 — Hera reveals stadium-sized asteroid crater from spacecraft impact
  Arriving at the site of humankind&#039;s first planetary defence experiment, the European Space Agency&#039;s Hera mission has found a miniature world of surprises.
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/space-safety/hera/hera-reveals-stadium-sized-asteroid-crater-left-after-spacecraft-impact
- Tue, 24 Nov 2026 09:00:00 +0100 — Euclid breaks own quasar record by 40 million years
  The European Space Agency’s Euclid space telescope has discovered the most distant quasar to date: a rare 360 million-sun-mass galactic core seen just 630 million years after the Big Bang. The discovery breaks Euclid’s own previous record, announced only a few months ago[1.1], by peering back in time an additional 40 million years. It also raises a compelling question: how could such an enormous black hole have formed and grown so rapidly after the Big Bang?
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/science/euclid/euclid-breaks-own-quasar-record-by-40-million-years
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 19:00:00 +0100 — BepiColombo safely in Mercury orbit despite unexpected challenge
  On 21 November, starting at 18:24 UTC, BepiColombo achieved Mercury orbit insertion (MOI), the second major milestone of its planetary arrival phase. As soon as the signal from the spacecraft was re-established after a scheduled loss of contact, the Mission Control Team at the European Space Operations Centre (ESOC) in Darmstadt, Germany, saw that the spacecraft had entered safe mode. An issue occurred during the thruster burn, causing the manoeuvre to end earlier than expected. Since then, teams at ESOC have been working around the clock with lead spacecraft manufacturer Airbus and colleagues across the joint ESA and Japan Aerospace Exploration Agency (JAXA) mission to investigate the issue, which has been traced to a software glitch. BepiColombo is safe and in a stable orbit around Mercu
  https://www.esa.int/articles/about-us/directorates/operations/bepicolombo-safely-in-mercury-orbit-despite-unexpected-challenge
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 15:10:46 +0100 — Hera’s Didymos asteroid in eclipse
  A solar eclipse in deep space, shown in this early view of the Didymos binary asteroids from the European Space Agency’s Hera mission for planetary defence. The smaller Dimorphos moonlet is seen casting its shadow across part of its Didymos parent body, to blank out the Sun across the shaded stretch of rocky surface.

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . Entre sus competencias clave se encuentran asegurar la acreditaci&oacute;n de seguridad para todas las acciones

- undated — BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto
  BepiColombo, inserimento in orbita con imprevisto &#8211; MEDIA INAF Dopo otto anni e 10,2 miliardi di chilometri percorsi, sabato 21 novembre 2026 la missione dell’Agenzia spaziale europea (Esa) BepiColombo è entrata in orbita attorno a Mercurio. A dare la notizia, un post su X pubblicato dalla stessa agenzia che confermava il completamento della manovra e la ricezione del segnale da parte della sonda. In un post successivo pubblicato il giorno seguente, però, la stessa agenzia ha chiarito che non tutto sarebbe andato come previsto: un&#8217;anomalia software avrebbe provocato l&#8217;interruzione anticipata dell&#8217;accensione dei propulsori, costringendo la sonda a entrare in modalità di sicurezza. In una comunicazione ufficiale pubblicata questa sera, infine, si legge che l’inserimento in orbita è comunque riuscito e che il veicolo si trova in un&#8217;orbita stabile attorno al pia
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/23/bepicolombo-inserimento-in-orbita-con-imprevisto/
- undated — Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai
  Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare. #### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

- undated — pdf
  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

  ## [Een mislukte lancering, een geslaagde landing en heel veel ruimtetelescopen Verder de mijlpaal van één lichtdag van de Voyager 1, een mogelijke nieuwe dwergplaneet - nog zonder naam - en een Chinese missie naar de Jupitermaan Callisto. Dat en meer bespreken Marieke Baan, Jeffrey Bout en Herbert Blankesteijn in deze nieuwe Space Cowboys. Schrijf je in voor de nieuwsbrief van Space Cowbows en krijg elke aflevering in je mailbox zodra deze uitkomt - op thysroes.nl/spacecowboys SpaceCowboys behandelt ruimtevaart- en astronomienieuws van land, planeet en daarbuiten. - undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 23 november 2026 Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE Lees meer
  Wat als…? Betere besluitvorming mogelijk met Digitale tweeling DestinE - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zeven jaar en vijfhonderd miljoen euro. Dat investeert de Europese Commissie in de ontwikkeling van de ‘digitale tweeling’ Destination Earth. Maar wat kun je met zo’n tweeling? En hoe onderscheidt hij zich van andere informatiediensten? Daarover ging de eerste nationale DestinE Living Lab dag in Amsterdam. &nbsp; Destination Earth ( DestinE ) is een digitale representatie van onze planeet. Een simulatie die de wisselwerking tussen natuur, klimaat en menselijk handelen op onze planeet zo nauwkeurig mogelijk nabootst. Dat doet hij op basis van verschillende databronnen – waaronder satellietdata van het Europese aardobservatieprogramma Copernicus – en met behulp van geavanceerde klimaat- en weermodellen, supercomputers en AI.&nbsp; ‘Satellietgegevens helpen ons te begrijpe
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nieuws/wat-als-betere-besluitvorming-mogelijk-met-digitale-tweeling-destine/

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha
  https://www.blick.ch/politik/vertrag-unterzeichnet-vbs-erhaelt-satellitenbilder-aus-frankreich/lv5p0cg

Eine Finanzierung kann verschiedene Formen annehmen, darunter direkte Zuschüsse für Forschungsprojekte, Unterstützung für die Entwicklung neuer Technologien und Förderung von Kooperationen zwischen Hochschulen und der Industrie. Die meisten Geschäftsmöglichkeiten mit der ESA werden über ESA STAR publication oder auf dem ESA Star Portal veröffentlicht. Die höheren Fachschulen stellen die Diplomzusätze mit Hilfe von Mustervorlagen des SBFI selbst aus. Nachträgliche Diplomzusätze müssen beim SBFI beantragt werden. Diplomzusätze Sobald ein Abschluss der höheren Berufsbildung im NQR Berufsbildung eingestuft ist, erhalten sämtliche Absolventinnen und Absolventen die Diplomzusätze gemeinsam mit ihren Fachausweisen und Diplomen. Musterdiplomzusätze für die eingestuften Abschüsse stehen im Berufsverzeichnis zur Verfügung. Eidgenössische Prüfungen (eidg. Fachausweis und eidg. - Tue, 24 Nov 2026 15:03:00 +0100 — Astronomie: Weltraumteleskop »Euklid« entdeckt Quasar aus der Frühzeit des Universums
  Ein Team um Julien Wolf vom Max-Planck-Institut für Astronomie in Heidelberg hat einen Quasar nachgewiesen, dessen Strahlung aus der Zeit rund 630 Millionen Jahre nach dem Urknall stammt. Das ist ein Rekord.

  2018 haben Europa und Japan ein Orbiterpaar losgeschickt, um den sonnennahen Planeten Merkur zu erforschen. Nun ist »BepiColombo« in die Umlaufbahn eingetreten. Bald sollen sich die beiden Satelliten trennen und Daten erheben.

  Vor vier Jahren rammt eine NASA-Sonde den kleinen Mond eines Asteroiden. Nun ist eine europäische Raumsonde am Tatort eingetroffen, um den Schaden zu besichtigen. Eine erste Überraschung gibt es bereits.

  Erstmals schickt China mit einem Raumfahrer aus Pakistan einen Ausländer zu seiner Raumstation. Es ist nicht die einzige Neuerung bei der „Shenzhou 24“-Mission. Was erwartet die Männer im All?

- undated — FONDS Fonds privés au Luxembourg: souplesse encadrée Oliver Zwick & Maren Stadler-Tjan • 10:30 Contenu sponsorisé
  Fonds privés au Luxembourg: souplesse encadrée | Paperjam News Pour les gestionnaires de fonds privés, le Luxembourg permet d’adapter les structures dans un environnement réglementaire prévisible. Deux évolutions récentes en offrent une illustration concrète. Octroi de prêts et structuration : davantage de possibilités, sans alléger l’encadrement L’AIFMD II soumet les activités de prêt des fonds d’investissement alternatifs (FIA) à un régime européen harmonisé. Le Luxembourg l’a transposée par la loi du 3 mars 2026, généralement applicable depuis le 16 avril 2026, échéance européenne de transposition. L’UE reconnaît expressément une activité déjà permise au Luxembourg, sous réserve des exigences applicables. Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte

Svoboda je astronautem v záloze a teď mu svitla reálná naděje, že se do vesmíru skutečně podívá v rámci některé z misí Axiom. Jeho cestu podporuje vláda i premiér Petr Fiala.   Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe&#8217;s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. - undated — Aerospacelab otworzył największą w Europie fabrykę satelitów
  Aerospacelab otworzył największą w Europie fabrykę satelitów Menu 24 listopada br. odbyło się oficjalne otwarcie nowego zakładu produkcyjnego firmy Aerospacelab pod belgijskim Charleroi. Megafabryka, której budowa kosztowała około 100 mln euro, zajmuje powierzchnię 20 tys. m² i docelowo ma produkować do 500 satelitów rocznie. Czyni to z obiektu nie tylko największą fabrykę satelitów w Europie, ale także trzecią co do wielkości na świecie. Rozpoczęcie produkcji zaplanowano na początek przyszłego roku. Początkowo zakład ma zatrudniać około 500 pracowników, natomiast do końca 2027 r. firma zamierza zwiększyć zatrudnienie do około tysiąca osób. Creotech liderem polsko-belgijskiego projektu dla programu IRIS² Produkowanie niemal półtora satelity dziennie ma być możliwe dzięki zastosowaniu produkcji taśmowej, silnie inspirowanej przemysłem motoryzacyjnym. - undated — Sondin pristanek na… Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig ko…
  Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg.   Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

Running from 10 to 13 November 2026, the 4-day mission is organized by the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) in collaboration with the Italian Space Agency (ASI) and the Kenya Space Agency (KSA). The event brings together international experts and representatives from African space institutions across 16 nations to strengthen regional capacity in space law and policy. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. - undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos.   ![Image 1](https://crts.gov.ma/img/logo.png) ###### PROJET SCO IRRISAT ## GESTION OPTIMALE DE L'EAU DESTINEE A L'IRRIGATION Le projet SCO IRRISAT, une initiative novatrice qui vise à répondre aux défis liés à la gestion optimale de l'eau agricole au Maroc, en particulier dans les régions semi-arides. Face à l'augmentation de la demande en eau due à l'extension de l'agriculture et aux sécheresses récurrentes, SCO IRRISAT propose une approche novatrice basée sur l'observation de la Terre. [Voir plus](https://crts.gov.ma/sco_irrisat.html) ###### Image Satellite ## Port Casablanca Maroc Exploration fascinante du port de Casablanca, figé dans le temps. Cette vue aérienne offre un aperçu spectaculaire de l'activité portuaire, témoignant de l'importance stratégique de cette plaque tournante maritime. [Plus d'images](https://crts.gov.ma/#) ###### Image Satellite ## Station Noor Ouarzazate Plongez dans l'éclat captivant de la Station Noor à Ouarzazate. Une merveille d'ingénierie solaire, cette image révèle la splendeur technologique et la contribution majeure de cette station à la transition énergétique, capturant l'essence même de l'innovation marocaine.   NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

Home / Hosting of 20 th International Committee on GNSS (ICG-20), Goa, India. November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. The meeting had in-person participation of 263 delegates, including 176 foreign delegates from 20 countries, and online participation of 84 delegates. The delegates i
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Hosting_of_20th_International_Committee_on_GNSS.html

  Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully Home / Ground test of third stage of PSLV (PS3) conducted successfully November 12, 2026 The ground static test of the PSLV third stage (PS3) was carried out successfully at the Static Test Facility in Satish Dhawan Space Centre, Sriharikota on November 12, 2026. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far. - undated — ส่องสตาร์ตอัปด้าน “ธุรกิจอวกาศ” ของไทย ไม่แพ้ชาติใดในโลก
  ส่องสตาร์ตอัปด้าน “ธุรกิจอวกาศ” ของไทย ไม่แพ้ชาติใดในโลก Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation ส่องสตาร์ตอัปด้าน “ธุรกิจอวกาศ” ของไทย ไม่แพ้ชาติใดในโลก By กัญญาภัค ทิศศรี By กัญญาภัค ทิศศรี NIA ดันสตาร์ตอัปสายอวกาศ ผ่าน “Space Economy: Lifting Off 2022” พร้อมอวดโฉมน้องใหม่ของวงการอวกาศ ที่มาพร้อมโซลูชั่นรุกไทยให้เป็นผู้ผลิตเทคโนฯ ลดการพึ่งพาจากต่างชาติ ก้าวสู่ ยุคเทคโนโลยีอวกาศ กับธุรกิจแห่งอนาคต มาทำความรู้จักกับคำว่า &ldquo; เทคโนโลยีอวกาศ &rdquo; หรือที่เราคุ้นหูกันบ่อย ๆ ก็คือ &ldquo; Space Tech &rdquo; แท้จริงแล้วมันคืออะไร แล้วจะนำไปต่อยอดในธุรกิจได้อย่างไร&nbsp; มาค้นหาคำตอบไปพร้อมกันจากโครงการ &ldquo; Space Economy : Lifting Off 2022 &rdquo; หนึ่งในกิจกรรมจากสำนักงานนวัตกรรมแห่งชาติ ( NIA ) ที่ดำเนินการร่วมกับภาคีความร่วมมืออวกาศไทยเพื่อผลักดันเศรษฐกิจอวกาศให้เกิดขึ้น ด้วยการพัฒนาผู้ประกอบการด้านเทคโนโลยี อวกาศ ให้มีบทบาทสามารถเติบโตได้ในอุตสาหกรรมอวกาศ และลดการนำเข้าเทคโนโลยีจากต่างประเ

  โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล Login หน้าแรก Tech Innovation โอกาสอนาคตเศรษฐกิจอวกาศ | ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา By ภัณณิน สุมนะเศรษฐกุล | สถาบันอนาคตไทยศึกษา วิสัยทัศน์อนาคตด้านอวกาศ ครอบคลุมทั้งการปกป้องโลกให้ดีขึ้น การนำประโยชน์จากอวกาศมาใช้งานในโลก รวมทั้งการแสวงหาความเป็นไปได้อื่นๆ ที่มนุษย์ยังไปไม่ถึง ทิศทางอนาคต จากผู้นำเอกชนระดับโลกด้านอวกาศอย่าง &ldquo; อีลอน มัสก์ &rdquo; ผู้ก่อตั้ง บริษัทสเปซเอ็กซ์ (SpaceX) วางวิสัยทัศน์ไปสู่การตั้งถิ่นฐานของมนุษย์โลกที่ดาวอังคาร ในขณะที่ผู้นำภาคธุรกิจอย่าง &ldquo; เจฟฟ์ เบซอส &rdquo; จาก บริษัทบลู ออริจิน (Blue Origin) มีแผนอนาคตที่จะทำให้ดวงจันทร์เป็นอาณานิคมแห่งใหม่ของโลก เพื่อตอบโจทย์ข้อท้าทายที่โลกไม่สามารถดำเนินการได้ จากวิสัยทัศน์อนาคตของผู้นำระดับโลกเหล่านี้ เป็นกุญแจสำคัญในการต่อยอดโอกาสของ เศรษฐกิจอวกาศที่นานาประเทศจับตามอง 5 แนวทาง ได้แก่ 1. การเดินทางสู่อวกาศ (Going to space)

#### Nation Thailand (press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year
  Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. - Thu, 26 Nov 2026 13:07:02 GMT — Defense minister calls for easing tension with Russia after air defense zone entry
  Defense Minister Kang Shin-chul on Thursday called for efforts to ease tensions with Russia, after Russian warplanes flew into Korea's air defense zone and almost breached its territorial airspace earlier this week. Two Russian Tu-160 supersonic strategic bombers and two other fighter jets entered Korea's air defense identification zone (KADIZ) in the East Sea early Tuesday. They came within about 1 kilometer of the airspace over Ulleung Island off the east coast, prompting the Korean Air Force to scramble fighter jets in response. Seoul has lodged a strong protest with the Russians over their repeated unannounced and unauthorized entry into KADIZ. "I believe we need to make efforts to ease tensions at this point," Kang said during a parliamentary session. "While we maintain communication
  https://www.koreatimes.co.kr/southkorea/defense/20261126/defense-minister-calls-for-easing-tension-with-russia-after-air-defense-zone-entry?utm_source=rss

- undated — 소행성발 ‘지구 종말’ 막는 실험 4년 뒤…충돌 행성 한쪽 ‘납작’ 인류 최초의 ‘지구 방위’ 실험 현장이 4년만에 모습을 드러냈다.소행성 충돌에 대비한 지구 방위 작전의 일환으로 시도했던 이 실험에서 우주선과 정면 충돌한 소행성은 한쪽 면이 납작하게 찌그러졌으며, 뒤뚱거리며 궤도를 돌고 있는 것으로 확인됐다. 또 소행성 주위엔 수백개...
  소행성발 ‘지구 종말’ 막는 실험 4년 뒤…충돌 행성 한쪽 ‘납작’ 본문 미래&amp;과학 과학 소행성발 ‘지구 종말’ 막는 실험 4년 뒤…충돌 행성 한쪽 ‘납작’ 곽노필 기자 수정 2026-11-26 11:15 펼침 0:00 Your browser does not support the audio element. 곽노필의 미래창 유럽 탐사선 헤라, 디모르포스 첫 근접 촬영 소행성 한쪽 면 전체에 커다란 충돌구 생겨 유럽우주국의 헤라 우주선이 600km 거리에서 촬영한 디디모스-디모르포스 쌍소행성. 왼쪽 디모르포스의 앞면이 4년 전 다트 우주선 충돌로 생긴 거대한 충돌구로 인해 납작해진 걸 알 수 있다. 유럽우주국 제공 광고 인류 최초의 ‘지구 방위’ 실험 현장이 4년만에 모습을 드러냈다. 소행성 충돌에 대비한 지구 방위 작전의 일환으로 시도했던 이 실험에서 우주선과 정면 충돌한 소행성은 한쪽 면이 납작하게 찌그러졌으며, 뒤뚱거리며 궤도를 돌고 있는 것으로 확인됐다. 또 소행성 주위엔 수백개의 바위가 널려 있었다. 유럽우주국(ESA)은 25일(현지 시각) 소행성 탐사선 헤라가 쌍소행성계 디디모스와 디모르포스에 접근해 촬영한 첫 관측 사진을 공개했다. 광고 헤라 임무 책임자인 이안 카넬리는 이날 독일 베를린 자연사박물관에서 사진을 공개하며 “6600만년 전 소행성 충돌이 지구 생명의 역사를 바꿨다면, 오늘날 인류는 역사상 처음으로 그 비극을 막을 방법을 검증하고 있다”고 말했다. 미국항공우주국(나사)의 우주선과 충돌한 지름 151m의 디모르포스는 지구에서 현재 1억9350만㎞ 떨어진 곳에서 지름 730m의 소행성 디디모스 주위를 1.2km 거리에서 공전하는 작은 소행성이다. 이 크기의 소행성은 지구에 충돌할 경우 도시 전체를 파괴할 수 있는 위력을 지니고 있다. 광고 광고 2022년 9월
  https://www.hani.co.kr/arti/science/science_general/1289180.html

  Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special | RNZ Skip to main content space 25 November 2026 Josh Aoraki explains what made the Beaver Supermoon special From Morning Report , 8:51 am on 25 November 2026 It may have been worth staying up late, or setting an early alarm, to catch a glimpse of the Moon overnight. November's full moon reached 'peak illumination' just a couple of hours ago, and this one is a super moon - meaning it coincides with the Moon being at its closest point to Earth in its orbit. Known as a Beaver Supermoon, it's the first of three consecutive supermoons, with another due in December. So what made last night's moon so special? Astronomer Josh Aoraki spoke to John Campbell.
